## V1 Delete Boards Columns Cards Id Json

### v1_delete_boards_columns_cards_id_json

`v1_delete_boards_columns_cards_id_json`

Remove a Card

**Risk:** `destructive`

```ts
await corsair.teamwork.api.v1_delete_boards_columns_cards_id_json({
	path: { id: '1' },
	query: {},
});
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `path` | `object` | No | Defaults to &#123;&#125;. |
| `query` | `object` | No | Defaults to &#123;&#125;. |

<Accordion title="path full type">

```ts

  id: string
}
```
</Accordion>

<Accordion title="query full type">

```ts
{
}
```
</Accordion>
</AccordionGroup>



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `STATUS` | `string` | No | â€” |

## V3 Put Projects Api V3 Time Timelogid Json

### v3_put_projects_api_v3_time_timelogid_json

`v3_put_projects_api_v3_time_timelogid_json`

Update an existing time entry.

**Risk:** `write`

```ts
await corsair.teamwork.api.v3_put_projects_api_v3_time_timelogid_json({
	path: { timelogId: 1 },
	query: {},
	body: {
		timelog: { taskId: 1, isUtc: true },
		timelogOptions: {},
		tags: [{}],
	},
});
```
